               malaiya kulapati uccipōy
       iṭiya, imakiri eḻutu varicilai
               utiyar kuṭapula veṟpuvēr
       iḷaka, ikaliya varuṇaṉ uramiru
               piḷavu paṭaeṟi koṟkaiyōṉ
       muṭiya, muṉivuoḻi iravi! cayatara!
               mutalva! paṇimaṇi muttamē!
       muruka! nirupama! apaya ravikula
               tilaka! paṇimaṇi muttamē!

   49. taṉ vīrarkaḷiṉ kaḻalkaḷ olitta aḷavilē vaṭa puvimaṉṉar, vaṭukar,
   kaṭṭimāṉ, tayilaṉ, makataṉ, maccarkōṉ, malaiya kulapati, imayattil
   viṟpoṟi poṟitta cēraṉ, kaṭal cuvaṟa vēlviṭṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ ākiyōr tōṟṟaṉar,
   avarkaḷai veṉṟu kōpam nīṅkiṉavaṉ cūriyaṉ marapil utitta cayataraṉ.
   maṟaliya - etirtta; vaḻi-vamcam; vēr iḷaka - āṭṭaṅ koḷḷa; varuṇaṉ uram
   irupiḷavupaṭa eṟital - kaṭalil vēlviṭṭu ataṉaic cuvaṟac ceyta
   ukkirakumāraṉ ceyal. itaṉait tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇattāl aṟika. (5)
   ---------------------------------
   50.
   taṉaiyar palaroṭu tayilaṉ makaṉmakaṉ
               makaṉum aṉikamum muṭṭanēr
       cariya, vaṭapulam eriya, malainilai
               taḷara varutamiḻ viṟkuḻām
       eṉaiyar cilarpalar veruva, imaiyavar
               parava, irucuṭar tappavāy
       eriya aṟukuṟai ceṟiya, uṭaluku
               kuruti natietir cekkarvāṉ
       aṉaiya kaḷaṉiṭai ilaku tikirikaḷ
               iḷaka, mutaṉmutal kaṟṟavāḷ
       aḷavu mutukiri piḷavu paṭanaṭu
               aruvi varaipurai mattamā
       muṉaiyil naṭaviya kuricil! ceyatara!
               mutalva! paṇimaṇi muttamē!
       muruka! nirupama! apaya! ravikula
               tilaka! paṇimaṇi muttamē!

   50. aṉikam - cēṉai; muṭṭa - etirkka; viṟkuḻām - vilvīrar kūṭṭam.
   tamiḻvīrar cilarukku ēṉaiya nāṭṭu vīrar palar añciṉar eṉpatu. irucuṭar
   tappa vāy eriya -cūriyacantirar maṟaiyumāṟu pukaicūḻa appakkam neruppāl
   eriya; aṟukuṟai ceṟiya - aṟṟa kuṟai uṭalkaḷ mika. pōrkkaḷam
   kurutipperukkāl cevvāṉ pōṉṟatu. atil ceṉṟa tērkaḷiṉ cakkaraṅkaḷ
   taraiyil patintaṉa. matam coritalāl yāṉaikaḷ aruvi pāyum malaikaḷai
   ottaṉa. (6)
   ----------------------------------
   51.
   paḻiyil icaivaṉa paruva maḻaimukil
               urumu vakaiceṟi koṟṟavā!
       pavaṉa katiyiṉum muṭuka ratamviṭu
               paḻaiya valavaril uttamā!
       vaḻiyum eḻukaṭal malaiyar makaḷoṭu
               vaṭiya viṭumeḻu koṭṭinīr
       malarum eḻupoḻil varaiyiṉ makaḷiroṭu
               iṉitu makiḻtara vaittu,kār
       viḻiyiṉ vaḻivaravu oḻiyum vaṭapula
               virutar aṇiviḻa veṭṭi,āḷ
       viputar kulakiri muḻutum, eḻutiya
               vicaya valipeṟa naṭṭatūṇ
       moḻiyil, 'ivaṉetir evaṉkol' eṉavarum
               mutalva! paṇimaṇi muttamē!
       muruka! nirupama! apaya! ravikula
               tilaka! paṇimaṇi muttamē!

   51. paruvamēkam iṭiyōṭu paḻiyillāta pukaḻuṟa ivaṉ ceṅkōlāl peytatu.
   ivaṉ vāyuvēkattil tēr celuttum tērōṭṭikaḷil mēmpaṭṭavaṉ; eḻukaṭalum
   eḻupoḻilum makiḻtara vaittu, vaṭapula maṉṉar paṭaikaḷ cāya, vāṉavarkaḷ
   taṅkum kulakiri muḻutum veṟṟittūṇ nāṭṭit taṉakku oppavar illai eṉa
   varupavaṉ. virutar - paṭaivīrar; viputar - vāṉavar. (7)
   ------------------------
   52.
   karunaṭartu rukkarvaṭa kavucalarku kuttarviṭu
               kavuriyarma katattar piṟarcūḻ
       kaṭaleṉava ḷaittuamarar kakaṉatalam muṭṭa,oli
               kaḷiperuku kaḷatti ṉiṭaiyē,
       turakarata varkkanirai viraivaṉatu vaittu,varu
               tuṭiyaṭia ḷakkar niraiyē
       tuṇipaṭupi ṟakkanaṭuvu oṭimaṭima ruppiṇaikaḷ
               corikuruti muttam niṉaiyōm;
       urakaiyaṉa koṟkaiyaṉa utakaiyaṉa kacciyaṉa
               oḻukumaṇi muttam nayavōm;
       utayakiri attakiri imayakiri cakrakiri
               utirumvetir muttam ukavōm;
       aruntiyum akkaṭalum avaṉmaṭuvum meyppuyalum
               aruḷviḷaiyum muttam ukavōm;
       marakatani ṟattuapaya! maṉucarita! cakrataraṉ
               matalai!itaḻ muttam aruḷē!

   52. karunaṭar mutaliyōr cūḻntu kaṭaleṉa oliperukap poruta kaḷattil,
   cōḻaṉ, kutiraiyiṉattai aḻittu yāṉaikaḷait tuṇṭittu maruppukkaḷai
   oṭikkumpōtu, kurutiyōṭu veḷippaṭum muttukkaḷai virumpi niṉaiyōm; utakai
   mutaliya ūrkaḷil kiṭaikkum muttukkaḷai virumpōm. utayakiri
   mutaliyavaṟṟil utirum mūṅkil muttai ukavōm; avaṉ natiyum, kaṭalum,
   kuḷaṅkaḷum, avaṉ nāṭṭil maḻai peyyum mēkaṅkaḷum tarum muttukkaḷai
   virumpōm. cakkarattait tarikkum vikkiramaṉ kuḻantaiyē! nī muttam
   taruka.
   yāṉaittantam, kaṭal, mūṅkil, maḻainīr ākiya muttuppiṟakkum iṭaṅkaḷ
   ippāṭalil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. 'kattum taraṅkam' (tiruccentūr murukaṉ
   piḷḷaittamiḻ) eṉṟa pāṭalai nōkkuka. (8)
   -----------------------------------
   53.
   orupaṉaipa ḻuttutirum oḻukukaṉi poṟkaṉiyil
               ulakiṭar keṭuttatu aṟivōm;
       orupaṉaiki ḻittuataṉil uvariciṟu kapperuka
               orunati vakuttatu aṟivōm;
       murukukamaḻ kaccipuka muḻuvatumvi ḷakkaviti
               muṟaimaiyil aḻittatu aṟivōm;
       muḻukula natikkuaracar muṭikoṭu vakuttakarai
               mukiltoṭa amaittatu aṟivōm:
       irupuṟamum okkaniṉatu orupulipo ṟikkavaṭa
               imakiriti rittatu aṟivōm;
       ikalmukari mukkaṇilum orukaṇiḻi yakkiḻiyil
               eḻutukaṇ aḻittatu aṟivōm;
       varukalivi lakkuvaṉa ivaiivai koṭuttakatai
               vaḻivaḻi piṭippatu aṟivōm;
       marakatani ṟattuapaya! maṉucarita! cakrataraṉ
               matalai! itaḻ muttam aruḷē!

   53. niṉmuṉṉōr oppaṟṟa paṉaiyiliruntu paḻuttu utirum poṟkaṉiyāl
   ulakattuyar tuṭaittamai, kuṟukkāka irunta paṉaimarattai veṭṭik kāviri
   perukivara vaḻi vakuttamai (48), kāñcinakar muḻutum aḻittamai,
   kāvirikkup pakaiyaracaraik koṇṭu vāṉaḷāviya karai kaṭṭuvittamai,
   imayattu irupuṟamum puli poṟittamai, mukari eṉṟa maṉṉaṉ paṭattai eḻuti
   atilirunta mūṉṟāmkaṇṇai 'itu mikaikkaṇ'ṇeṉṟu vēlāl eṟintu uṇmaiyil avaṉ
   mūṉṟām kaṇṇai aḻittamai, vaṟumaiyaip pōkkap palarukkum palapala
   koṭuttamai ākiyavaṟṟai aṟivōm.
   attakaiya emakku nī muttam taruka.
   mukari paṟṟiya ceyti kaliṅkattup paraṇiyilum uḷḷatu. 'toḻutu maṉṉarē'
   eṉṉum tāḻicai nōkkuka. (9)
   ----------------------------------------
   54.
   veruvarummu kakkaṭalkaḷ varavarai rukkaayal
               viraitaruma larkkai taruvōm;
       viṭamoḻiya eppoḻutum amutukaṭai yakkaṭaiya
               viraivatuor aḷakkar taruvōm;
       urumeṉa muḻaṅkaeḻu murukiyama ḻaikkuatirvu
               ciṟupaṟaiyum takka taruvōm;
       urukavaṭi vittiviṭa veḷiṟuuraini ṟakkaṭavuḷ
               urukeḻuka lappai taruvōm;
       arunaṭamna ṭittuniṉatu aṟituyila ṟatticaiyil
               aravaraciṉ ucci taruvōm;
       aḻakoṭuti rukkaivaḷai tikirikaḷiṉ oppuṭaiya
               alarkatir iraṭṭai taruvōm;
       varicaiyiṉ aḷittaruḷa vaḷaikaḷ eṟikacciyiṉil
               maṟainitive ṟukkai taruvōm;
       marakatani ṟattu apaya! maṉucarita! cakrataraṉ
               matalai! itaḻ muttam aruḷē!

   54. añcattakka kaṭalkaḷ uṉ kaikaḷukkuḷ irukkumāṟu aṭakkit taruvōm.
   viṭam nīṅkak kaṭaiyak kaṭaiya amutamē nalkum pāṟkaṭalaiyum taruvōm.
   mēkattukkup pōṭṭiyāka iṭipōla olikkum ciṟupaṭai taruvōm. pakaippulattē
   kavaṭikaḷai vitaiyāka vitaikka, veḷḷai niṟattavaṉāṉa palarāmaṉuṭaiya
   āyutamāṉa añcattakka kalappaiyait taruvōm. nī aṟituyil nīṅki aḻakiya
   naṭaṉamāṭa āticēṭaṉuṭaiya talaiyākiya araṅkiṉait taruvōm. uṉ
   kaikaḷiluḷḷa caṅkucakkaraṅkaḷukku iṇaiyāṉa cantiracūriyarkaḷait
   taruvōm. varuvōrukku vaḻaṅkak kāñcinakaril potintu vaittirukkum
   celvattait taruvōm. cakrataraṉ matalaiyē! muttam taruka.
   veruvaru - añcattakka; aḷakkar - kaṭal; murukiya - mutirnta; maḻai -
   mēkam; takka - takutiyuṭaiyaṉa; kavaṭi - cōḻi; veḷiṟu urai niṟakkaṭavuḷ
   - palarāmaṉ; uru - accam; aravaracu - āticēṭaṉ; katir iraṭṭai - cantira
   cūriyar; vaḷaikaḷ eṟi - caṅkukaḷ mōtum; maṟai niti - putaiyal. (10)
   ---------------------------------------
   55.
   tuḷavukama ḻattaḻuvum muṟaipukutu ṟaikkumari
               kulaceḻiyar tattai peṟuvāḷ;
       curarulakam eṭṭumatu toḻukulamvi ḷakkavaru
               tuṟaikeḻuki ḻatti, peṟuvāḷ;
       iḷanilavue ṟittaṉaiya mukiḻnakaii ḻaitterivai
               eḻupuvaṉam mitrai peṟuvāḷ;
       iruḷ aṟavaḷaittuilaku tikiriyil iṭattaṉuvil
               iṉitua marumnittai peṟuvāḷ;
       aḷaviltalai peṟṟapi(ḷ)ḷait tamiḻmuḷari(i) taḻttacivil
               alakilpari cuttai peṟuvāḷ;
       alaikaṭalku ḷittapoḻutu aḻakiyapu yattileḻu
               parimitaivi tattai peṟuvāḷ;
       vaḷaaḷavui ṟapperiya paravaiaka lattumakiḻ
               vaḷarcakala muttai peṟuvāḷ;
       marakatani ṟattapaya! maṉucarita! cakrataraṉ
               matalai!itaḻ muttam aruḷē!

   55. tattai - muṉpiṟantāḷ; kiḻatti - uriyavaḷ; mitrai - tōḻi; nittai -
   umai (koṟṟavai); paricuttai - tūya veṇṇiṟattavaḷāṉa kalaimakaḷ;
   parimitai - alaṅkāram; vitattai - (mukaṭikkup) piṉ piṟantavaḷāṉa
   tirumakaḷ; cakalamuttai - cakalamityai - ulakat tōṟṟak kāraṇamāṉa
   pirakiruti cakti, nilamakaḷ. ceḻiyariṉ kumarittuṟaiyait tamakkaiyum,
   tēvalōkattai otta ciṟappuṭaiya cōṇāṭṭai uṉ tēviyum, eḻupuvaṉattaiyum
   nilāvai otta paṟkaḷai uṭaiya tōḻiyum, cakkaram vil ākiya iṭattaik
   koṟṟavaiyum, uṉ piḷḷaittamiḻai veṇṭāmarai vāḻ kalaimaka ḷum, kaṭalil
   kuḷittapoḻutu uṉ puyattil eḻum aḻakait tirumakaḷum, uṉ paranta mārpil
   makiḻvatai nilamakaḷum muṟaiyē peṟuvar eṉpatu. marakata niṟattaṉ
   vikkiramaṉ eṉpatum, pavaḷaniṟattaṉ kulōttuṅkaṉ (24) eṉpatum aṟika. (11)
   -------------------------------------

                           6. vārāṉaipparuvam.

   56.
   muṟukiya kaliyum muraṇiya aracum
               mukamalar karuka varukavē!
       muḷariyiṉ makaḷum muḻunila makaḷum
               muṟaimuṟai peruka varukavē!
       kuṟukiya pakaiñar kurutiyai niṉatu
               koṭuvari paruka varukavē!
       kuṭaiyuṭaṉ ikalum taḷirmati paḻaiya
               kuraikaṭal ceruka varukavē!
       iṟukiya makaram iṭaiiṭai tuṇiya,
               eḻuperu vayiṟum eyiṟumē
       ericeṟi pukaḻa, viṭucuṭi kaimiṉil
               eḻiliyum iriya, iriyalpōy
       maṟukiya varuṇaṉ vaḻipaṭa, mutuku
               vaḷaicilai apayaṉ! varukavē!
       maṉukula tilakaṉ ravikula tilakaṉ
               makaṉmakaṉ! varuka varukavē!

   vārāṉaip paruvam
   taḷarnaṭaiyiṭṭu varum kuḻantaiyait tammai nōkki varumāṟu tāyar
   aḻaippatākak kūṟuvatu ipparuvac ceyti. vārāṉai - varukai; āṉai -
   toḻiṟpeyar vikuti.
   56. vaṟumaiyum pakaiyaracum mukam karukavum, tirumakaḷum nilamakaḷum
   makiḻavum, aṇukiya pakaivar kurutiyai uṉ puli parukavum, uṉ kuṭai
   vaṉappai ottirukkum mati avvaṉappiṟkut tōṟṟuk kaṭalil maṟaiyavum,
   irāmāvatāra kālattil kaṭalil mīṉkaḷ tuṇṭaṅkaḷāka, kaṭal vayiṟu tīyiṉāl
   kotikka, nīr mukappataṟkuk kaṭalukku vanta mēkaṅkaḷ añci ōṭa, varuntiya
   varuṇaṉ vaḻipaṭumāṟu vil vaḷaitta perumāṉē! varuka. muṟukiya - koṭiya;
   muḷari - tāmarai; koṭuvari - puli; ikalutal - ottiruttal; iriya -
   añciōṭa; maṟukiya - maṉamvaruntiya; ravikula tilakaṉ - mutal
   kulōttuṅkaṉ. (1)
   ---------------------------
   57.
   iraikavar makaram matiniḻal katuva
               eṟikaṭal katuvu tuvaraivēḷ!
       ilaimuṉi umiḻva puraipurai maṟaiyil
               iṭutuṭar paṟiyum eḻiliyōṉ!
       uraivaḷar nilavum upaniṭam aruḷum
               ulakaṟi piṟavi yuṭaiya cēy!
       uṭaiya avaṉiyil uṭupati marapil
               uṟumavaṉ marukaṉ! varukavē!
       paravara makaḷir eḻupuṭa viyiṉum
               varum oru pavaṉi ayara,ītu
       akammakiḻ poḻutu puṉalara makaḷir
               eḻukaṭal kaḷiṉum iriya,ēḻ
       varaiara makaḷir maṉaitoṟum maṟuki
               maṉammuḻutu uruka, varukavē!
       maṉukula tilakaṉ ravikula tilakaṉ
               makaṉ makaṉ! varuka varukavē!

   57. makara mīṉkaḷ cantiraṉ niḻalai uṇaveṉṟu kavarumāṟu kaṭaliṭaiyē
   amaikkappaṭṭa tuvārakait talaivaṉē! pakaivaruṭaiya malaikaḷai aḻikkum
   ampumāriyāl kārmēkam pōṉṟavaṉē! upaniṭatam kūṟumāṟu piṟaviyaiyuṭaiya
   talaivaṉē! cantirakula maṉṉaṉākiya pāṇṭiyaṉ marukaṉē! uṉṉai vaṇaṅkum
   mēmpaṭṭa ulaka makaḷir ēḻulakilum pavaṉi vantu makiḻa, atu poṟātu
   nīriluḷḷa aramakaḷir kaṭalkaḷil maṟaiya, malaikaḷiluḷḷa vāṉ aramakaḷir
   tattam iruppiṭaṅkaḷil varunti maṉam uruka, varuvāyāka.
   tuvārakai kaṭaliṉ naṭuvē kaṇṇaṉāl putitāka amaikkappaṭṭa ūr. katuvutal
   - kaippaṟṟutal; uṭupati - cantiraṉ. (2)
   paravuvara makaḷir - paravara makaḷir; tokuttal vikāram. uṉṉai
   vaḻipaṭum mēmpaṭṭa makaḷir eṉṟavāṟu.
   -----------------------------------
   58.
   uyir ilkaru nāṭar uṭalkaḷ naṭamāṭa,
               urakarpaku vāyiṉ urimaiyōṭu
       utiyarkuti pāya, utakaieḻa vēva
               uvaritirai tīya, uṭa liṉōr
       ceyalilkuṇa vāyil uṭaiya, iḻivāla
               aṭaiyamalai nāṭu vakaiyilē
       citaiya,muṉivu āṟum iravitaṉa tātaṉ
               maruka!ceka tīraṉ! varukavē!
       ayariṉmaṇi vīti acurarpuram mēru
               aṉaiyatoru tūṇiṉ iṭaieḻā,
       avaṉimuta lāya ulakupati ṉālum
               aracutaṉi āḷum avuṇarkōṉ
       vayiravarai ākam naṭuvupiḷa vāka
               vakirumuki rāḷi! varukavē!
       malaiyamalai vāṇaṉ, imayamalai āḷi!
               vaḷavaṉnakar īcaṉ! varukavē!

   58. karunāṭar uṭal tuṇṭamāki acaiya, cērar urimaimakaḷir pāmpukaḷiṉ
   vāyil puka, utakai vēva, kaṭal tīya, kuṇavāyil citaiya malaināṭṭai
   aḻittuk kōpam nīṅkiya cūriyakula maṉṉaṉ paramparaiyiṉaṉē! acurar
   kōnakaril tūṇiṭait tōṉṟi iraṇiyaṉ mārpai ukirāl piḷantavaṉē!
   malaiyamalait talaiva! imayamalait talaiva! cōḻaṉuṭaiya kōnakarukkut
   talaiva! varuka.
   pakuvāy - piḷanta vāy; urimai - maṉaivi; uṭaliṉōr - pakaivar; tātaṉ -
   pāṭṭaṉ; marukaṉ - vaḻit tōṉṟal; ukir āḷi - nakaṅkaḷai uṭaiya ciṅkam;
   āḷi - āḷpavaṉ. (3)
   -------------------------------
   59.
   takaramaṇam nāṟu toṭaiyalvaṭa nāṭar
               porutu,kuṭa kūṭal amarilē
       taraṇitara, vēṟum uṭaiyakavi rācar
               paraṇitara, mītu taṉimaikūr
       cikaravaṭa mēru kiriyaimati yōṭum
               naḷiṉapati yōṭum ikaliōr
       tikirivara, ēvu kuricilkari kālaṉ
               maruka!ceka tīcaṉ! varukavē!
       pakarauyar cūta paṉacaṭala vēri
               vaṉacanaṟa vōṭu parukavē,
       pakaṭupaṭu vāṉa mukaṭutoṭa vāvu
               pariyaperu vāḷai, vayalpukā,
       makaralaya vēṟu palavummika mūri
               valiyatiru nāṭaṉ! varukavē!
       malaiyamalai vāṇaṉ! imayamalai āḷi!
               vaḷavaṉ nakar īcaṉ! varukavē!

   59. takaram - mayirccāntu, vācaṉaip paṇṭam; toṭaiyal - malai;
   naḷiṉapati - cūriyaṉ; cūtam - māmaram; paṉacam - īrappalā; vaṉacam -
   tāmarai; pakaṭu - perumai; makaram - cuṟāmīṉ; vaya ēṟu - valiya āṇ;
   mūri - erutu; vaṭanāṭar taraṇi tara, kavirācar paraṇi pāṭa, cūriya
   cantirar cuṟṟum mērumalaiyait taṉ āṇaic cakkaramum cuṟṟi vara ēviya
   kuricil karikālaṉ. mā, palā ivaṟṟiṉ cāṟṟait tāmaraiyiṉ tēṉōṭu parukiya
   vāḷai, vāṉaḷāvak kutittu vayalil puka, cuṟāmīṉ palavum avaṟṟōṭu mika,
   valiya kāḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya nāṭaṉē! varuka. (4)
   ------------------------------
   60.
   kaṭaluṭaiya pūmi muḻutumoru nēmi
               kaṟuvupakai tīra niṟuviyē,
       kakaṉaneṟi ūṭu kavariiru pālum
               acaivacaiya, ōru kavikaiyōṭu,
       aṭaluṭaiya tāṉai amararpuṭai cūḻa,
               aramakaḷir pāṭa, avuṇarmēl
       aṭarviḻia nēkam uṭaiyaviṭai ūrum
               aracaṉvaḻi vīraṉ! varukavē!
       paṭaluṭaiya vāyil payilmataku kāval
               aḻakukulai vāṇar maṟaipōru
       patapateṉa mēti paṭukarviṭa vāvi
               paṭiyilveṭi pōya paruvarāl
       maṭaluṭaiya pūka vaṉamiṭaṟu nūṟi
               varaukaḷum nāṭaṉ! varukavē!
       malaiyamalai vāṇaṉ! imayamalai āḷi!
               vaḷavaṉnakar īcaṉ! varukavē!

   60. nēmi - āṇaiccakkaram; kakaṉam - ākāyam; aṭar viḻi anēkam uṭaiya
   viṭai - āyiraṅ kaṇṇaṉākiya intiraṉ kakuntaṉ eṉṟa aracaṉukku
   vākaṉamākiya kāḷai; pūkam - pākku; miṭaṟu – kaḻuttu; mēṟpakuti.
   pakai tīra nila ulakait taṉ āṇaikku uṭpaṭutti, vāṉa vaḻiyē ceṉṟu,
   aramakaḷir pāṭa amarar kuṭai kavittuk kavari vīca intiraṉākiya kāḷai
   mītu amarntu, avuṇarōṭu pōriṭṭu veṉṟa kakuttaṉ marapil vantavaṉ.
   matakukaḷiṉ kāvalaiyum kaṭantu erumaikaḷ kuḷaṅkaḷil pāya, anta
   atircciyāl vāḷaikaḷ pākkumaraṅkaḷiṉ kaḻuttu oṭiyat tāvik kutikkum
   nāṭaṉ. (5)
   -----------------------------------
   61.
   viṭuvaṭuvu mīṉa vicayamuṭi cūṭi
               aracutaṉi āḷum arumaiyōr
       viḷaivoḻiya, vāṉam maḻaipoḻiya, vēta
               vitivaḷarum mātar vilaiyilpūṇ
       aṭunaṭuvu kūrum iṭar oḻiya, ñālam
               aṭaiyaiṭar tīra aruḷiṉāṉ,
       apayaṉ!matu rēcaṉ! upayakula tīpaṉ!
               akilakali kōpaṉ! varukavē!
       naṭunaṭuvu nēyar makaḷiroṭu ñāḷa
               naḷiṉavaḷa vāvi natikaḷāl,
       nalivupaṭu vāḷai mutupakaṭu rāku
               varuvateṉa vāvu natiyatuōr
       maṭunaṭuu lāva, aṭunatuitu pōtu
               mativeruvu nāṭaṉ! varukavē!
       malaiyamalai vāṇaṉ! imayamalai āḷi!
               vaḷavaṉnakar īcaṉ! varukavē!

   61. paṇṭu aracāṇṭa pāṇṭiyar ceyal oḻiya avartam kuṟṟamaṟṟa mīṉ
   poṟiyiṉaiyuṭaiya muṭi cūṭi, paruvamaḻai poḻiya, vētaneṟi taḻaikka,
   makaḷir maṅkaliyattukku iṭar nīṅka, ulaku tuyar nīṅka aruḷiṉavaṉ
   apayaṉ. nēyamakaḷiroṭu koṭikaḷ paṭarnta tāmaraik kuḷaṅkaḷilum
   natikaḷilum muḻukuvatāl calaṉamuṟṟa vāḷaikaḷ, irāku eṉṟa kōḷ mēl nōkkic
   celvatupōla mēl nōkkippāya, taṉṉai irāku varutta varuvatākak karutic
   cantiraṉ añcum nāṭaṉ.
   viṭu vaṭuvu - kuṟai nīṅkiya; arumaiyōr - pāṇṭiyar; vilaiyil pūṇ -
   tirumaṅkaliyam; ñāḷam - paṭar koṭi. taṇṭu; aṭunatu itupōtu - ippoḻutu
   varuttuvatu. (6)
   ----------------------------------
   62.
   tiruṭuporuḷ mūkar, paḻaiyamoḻi yāḷar,
               veḷiyariḷa nīrar, iḷamaiyē
       tikaḻumoḻi yātar ivarkaḷoru nālvar
               ciṟitummati yātu, pararkaḷē
       neruṭuvaṉa tīra nikarilkavi rācaṉ
               nilavukavi mālai kulavutōḷ
       nirupaṉmakaṉ! mōkaṉ! naṭaṉ!naṭavi nōtaṉ!
               nirupakula tīpaṉ! varukavē!
       muruṭupaṭu mūri akiliṉmukil ēṟi
               muralummutu nēri mutukiṉmēl
       muṟukuvaṉa cōti kuṟukuvaṉa kūci
               murukukamaḻ cāral arukupōy,
       varuṭaikuti pāya naṭavunati tōṟum
               matitiṭaṟum nāṭaṉ! varukavē!
       malaiyamalai vāṇaṉ! imayamalai āḷi!
               vaḷavaṉ nakar īcaṉ! varukavē!

   62. poruḷ tiruṭum vaṟiyavar, paḻamperumaiyē pēcuvōr, aṟivilikaḷ,
   aṟivukkuruṭar ivarkaḷai viṭuttuc cāṉṟōraiyē pāṭum kaviyaracar tam
   pāmālaikaḷaic cūṭiya vikkiramaṉ makaṉ! varuka. kāyntu vaḷainta
   akilmarattiṉ ucciyil ēṟi mukilkaḷ muḻaṅkum nērimalaiyiṉ maṇam kamaḻum
   cāralil varuṭaimāṉkaḷ kutikkum natikaḷ, aṟivait tiṭukkiṭacceyyum nāṭaṉ!
   varuka. mūkar - vaṟiyavar; veḷiyar - nuṭpamāṉa aṟivu aṟṟavar; ātar -
   kuruṭar; muruṭu - viṟaku; muruku - naṟumaṇam; varuṭai - māṉ vicēṭam;
   mati - aṟivu; tiṭaṟutal – tiṭukkiṭal. (7)
   ---------------------------
   63.
   kuṟukukaḻal vīrar porutupaṭum īḻam
               uṭaiya,eḻu nūṟum aṭaiya,mī
       kuṭavutirai nīriṉ mutiyakaṭa lūṭu
               putiyakaṭal kāṇum iravicūṭu
       aṟukuceṟi kōṭi aracarmuṭi tōṟum
               aṇiyumapi ṭēkam aṇiyatāḷ
       apayaṉ!matu rēcaṉ! upayakula tīpaṉ!
               aṉupamaṉ!u lōkaṉ! varukavē!
       muṟukupuṉai ēya kaḷariviri kāḷam
               muṭiyumnilai pēṟu vaḻutipōy
       mutiyavarai ēṟi urimaiyuṭaṉ, vāṉa
               mukaṭutoṭu kōyil muḻutumē,
       maṟukupala kāṇa, maturaieṟi cōḻaṉ
               matalai!kali kōpaṉ! varukavē!
       malaiyamalai vāṇaṉ! imayamalai āḷi!
               vaḷavaṉnakar īcaṉ! varukavē!

   63. kuṭavu - vaḷainta; apiṭēkam - muṭi; aṇiya - aṇikalaṉākak koṇṭa;
   muṟuku - mutircci; kāḷam - eṭṭimaram; maṟukutal - varuntutal.
   paḻaiya kaṭalūṭu putiya kaṭal celvatu pōlac cēṉaikkaṭalaic celutti
   īḻanāṭṭai veṉṟu, aracar tam muṭikaḷ taṉ aṭikku aṇikaḷākac ceyta apayaṉ!
   varuka. mutirnta eṭṭimarak kāṭukaḷai uṭaiya paḻaiya malai mītu taṉ
   maṉaiviyuṭaṉ muṭiyum nilaiyai aṭainta pāṇṭiyaṉ ēṟa, avaṉatu maturai
   mānakara araṇmaṉaiyaip palavakait tuyaraṅkaḷaiyum āṇṭuḷḷār eytumāṟu
   aḻitta vikkiramaṉ makaṉ! varuka. (8)
   ---------------------------------
   64.
   ēṟumoru paṉṟiyai nakaṅkoṭu tuṭakkiyatu;
               iraṇṭaṭi iraṇṭua ṭiyiṭā
       ēḻaieti riṭṭakayaṉ mīṉaiyum iṭaṅkaraiyum
               aḷḷiya; eyiṟṟa pakuvāy
       ūṟupaṭa nontupiḷi ṟikkaḷiṟu kavviyum,
               muṟukkiyum muṟukkiyum nilattu
       ūḻipala vantupakai mēḻiyaiyum vīṇaiyaiyum
               muṟṟumiṭai cuṟṟum oruvāl;
       kīṟumuru mukkural oṭuṅka ulakaṅkaḷum
               naṭuṅkavum irunta kuralāl;
       ceṉṟuvaṭa mērukiri yiṟcikara vucciyil
               irukkumitu; eṉṟu ticaiyā
       māṟupaṭu veṅkali pulikku veruvappuvi
               purakkum uravōṉ! varukavē!
       vañciyum uṟantaiyum marantaiyum makōtaiyum
               aḷikkum apayaṉ! varukavē!

   64. ippāṭalil ivaṉukku muttiraiyā yamainta puliyiṉ ceyal kaṟpaṉaiyākak
   kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paṉṟiyai nakattāl aḻittu, iraṇṭu muṟai tāviya aḷavil
   kayal mīṉaiyum mutalaiyaiyum tukaittu, vāyiṉāl yāṉaiyaik kauvit
   tuṉpuṟutti, kalappai, vīṇai eṉpaṉavaṟṟai vāliṉāl cuṟṟi, taṉ kuralāl
   iṭiyōcaiyai oṭukki, mērumalai ucciyil vīṟṟirunta ivaṉuṭaiya
   pulippoṟikku añci vaṟumai taṭumāṟa, ivaṉ puvipurappavaṉ. paṉṟi, mīṉ,
   mutalai, yāṉai, kalappai, vīṇai, iṭi eṉpaṉa piṟa nāṭṭavar koṭikaḷ
   poṟikaḷ ivaṟṟiluḷḷa ciṉṉaṅkaḷ. (9).
   ----------------------------------
   65.
   paṭṭuttaraṇi paraṇik kavitara, oruvicai
               pakirati natiyaḷa vumpala ticaivica yattaṟi
       naṭṭut tamatuima kirikaṇ ṭaruḷiya narapati
               nakaravāḻ vikrama cōḻaṉ tirumakaṉ! iṉitaḷi
       iṭṭup poḻimaḻai iṭappeyal tokupuṭai atirkural
               etiriṭṭu aracar iḷavetir atirtalum, iṟuvarai
       maṭṭut taṭamiṭi vaṭaveṟpu uṭaiyavaṉ! varukavē!
               vaḷavaṉ! pañcavaṉ! utiyaṉ! cempiyaṉ!
       vayavaṉ! kuṇaparaṉ! varukavē!

   65 pulavar paraṇipāṭa, kaṅkaivaraiyiluḷḷa pakaivar nāṭukaḷ aḻiya, pala
   ticaikaḷilum veṟṟittūṇ nāṭṭi imayamalaiyai aṭippaṭuttiyavaṉ
   vikkiramacōḻaṉ. paruvamaḻai tavaṟātu peyya, avveḷḷam ōṭivarum oliyai
   ivaṉatu muracoliyākak karutip pakaimaṉṉarkaḷ uyirpiḻaikka malai nōkki
   ōṭukiṉṟaṉar. cempiyaṉ - cōḻaṉ. (10)
   -------------------------------
   66.
   uyirā tiyumematu uṭalā tiyumuṉ ṉiṉaṉeṉa,
               oḷiperu kuṅkō viyarkuṭai uṭai nīr uṭaiyiṉil
       tayirā caiyiṉetir taḻuvum ciṟumukil! varukavē!
               taṇṭā rakukula taṉinā yakaṉmakaṉ! varukavē!
       ayirā patakiri etirā varaviṭṭa tayilaṉum
               aṭaiyat taṉpaṭai vayirat tōḷ tuṇai kavarntueḻa,
       vayirā karakiri murivit tamakaṉ! varukavē!
       vaḷavaṉ! pañcavaṉ! utiyaṉ! cempiyaṉ!
               vayavaṉ! kuṇaparaṉ! varukavē!

   66. tamakku uyirākavum uṭalākavum kaṇṇaṉ irukkiṟāṉ eṉṟu makiḻum kōviyar
   uṭaiyait tayiraik kavarvatu pōlak kavarnta mukilē! varuka. taṉ paṭṭattu
   yāṉaiyākiya ayirāvatattai etirttu vanta tayilaṉuṭaiya paṭaivaliyai
   aḻittu, vayirākarattil yāṉai īṭṭaṅkaḷai aḻitta vikkiramaṉ makaṉē!
   varuka.
   paṭṭattuyāṉai ayirāvatattiṉ ciṟappu mūvarulāvil (vik. ulā) parakkak
   kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (11)
   -----------------------------------

                              7. ampulipparuvam

   67.
   kōḻikā valaṉivaṉ ceṅkaṇmāl kāṇ;makō
               tatikaḷē ḻumku ḻampik
       kuḻaviyāy niṉṉiṉum kuḷirumam pulikaḷē
               koṇṭukoṇṭu uṟavu koḷḷum
       pāḻikāṇ; vantaip periyavaṟku iṉiyaiyāy
               niṟpatē; niṉku lattup
       pañcavaṉa kuṭaiyoṭum paṭaiyoṭum porutuakap
               paṭṭanāḷ amalaṉ aṟivaṉ;
       ūḻikā laṅkulāy nilavueṟik kiṉṟatāl;
               veyileṟik kiṉṟa kālam
       uṉatucan tirōtayat tiṉakarō tayamum uṇṭu
               eṉṉil,aṅku oṉṟu caṅkuoṉṟu
       āḻikāṇiya; kiṭantu alamarātu ivaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   ampulip paruvam
   67. cantiraṉaik kuḻantaiyōṭu viḷaiyāṭavā eṉṟu aḻaippatu ipparuvacceyti.
   uṟaiyūr maṉṉaṉākiya ivaṉ tirumāl; periyakaṭalkaḷ ēḻum kuḻampa, uṉṉiṉum
   kuḻantaiyākattōṉṟum ampulikaḷ ivaṉōṭu uṟavu koḷḷum valimai uṭaiyavaṉ.
   ivaṉukku iṉimaiyāka iru. uṉ kulattup pāṇṭiyaṉ ivaṉōṭu paṭaiyum kuṭaiyum
   koṇṭu porutu ciṟaippaṭṭāṉ, ivaṉ kaiccaṅku cantiraṉ oḷiyaiyum, cakkaram
   cūriyaṉ oḷiyaiyum pallūḻik kālamāka veḷippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. maṉam
   taṭumāṟātu ivaṉōṭu viḷaiyāṭa vā. aṉupamaṉ - oppaṟṟavaṉ.
   ippāṭalil cantiraṉiṉum cōḻaṉ mikkāṉ eṉṟa ceyti kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu
   nālvakai upāyaṅkaḷuḷ pētamākum. (1)
   -----------------------------------
   68.
   cūḻiyā ṉaippuṟattu utaiyaṇaṉ, cōḻatuṅ
               kaṉtirut tampi, tūya
       tuṇaivaṉā kiyaceyaṅ koṇṭacō ḻaṟku iḷaṅ
               kōmakaṉ, tumpa ḷattuem
       mēḻiyāl iravupōl irācarā caṉkulak
               kaḷiṟukāṇ; ivaṉmuṉ inta
       veṟpilvā cavaṉnaka rapperumpuyal viḷak
               kumpirāṉ, vēlai kāṇīr!
       tāḻiyāy amutiṭat taṅkiḷai ceytataṟku,
               uṅkaḷaip pukavi ḻuṅkit
       taḻalviḻittu etirvarum irākukē tukkatu
               viṭa,uṭal iṭamatu aṟa,vēntu
       āḻiyāl aṟaeṟin taruḷilōṉ teriyumō?
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli āṭa vāvē!

   68 cūḻi - yāṉaiyiṉ mukapaṭām; vācavaṉ - intiraṉ. ivaṉ nāṭṭil
   intiraṉaruḷāl paruvamaḻai tavaṟātu peykiṟatu.
   aṅku iḷai ceytal - amutam pakirntaḷitta iṭattuk kāval ceytal. cūriya
   cantirarai viḻuṅkum irākukētukkaḷai veruṭṭi ivarkaḷait tirumāl kāppāṉ
   eṉpatu. 'kōḷvāy matiyam neṭiyāṉ viṭuttāṅku' (cīvaka. 456) eṉṟār
   piṟarum.
   pāṟkaṭaliṉiṉṟum amutam tōṉṟiyapiṉ, mōkiṉi avatāram koṇṭu acurarai
   mayakki vañcittuviṭṭut tirumāl tēvarkaḷukku avvamutattai akappaiyāl
   koṭuttuvarukaiyil, cimmikaiyiṉ putalvaṉāṉa caimmikēyaṉ acura uruvattaik
   karantu tēvar urukkoṇṭu avvamutattaip peṟṟāṉāka, cūriya cantirar
   avvacuraṉaik kāṭṭikkoṭuttārāka, tirumāl amirtam pakirntu koṭutta
   akappaiyiṉal avvacuraṉait tākka, avaṉ talaivēṟu uṭalvēṟu
   piḷavupaṭṭāṉāyiṉum amutuṇṭamaiyāl uyir nīṅkātu niṉṟu irāku kētukkaḷ
   eṉṟu iruvaṭivākip peyarpūṇṭu, kōḷ coṉṉa cūriyacantirarkaḷaic cila
   kālaṅkaḷil paṟṟikkoḷḷa, avarkaḷait tirumāl paṟṟukkōḷiṉiṉṟum
   viṭuvikkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu purāṇam kūṟum.
   cantiraṉ tuyar kāttamai tāṉam. (2)
   ------------------------------------
   69.
   maṇiniḻal kuṉṟamyā vaiyumaṭip poṭipaṭar
               vaṉpiṇak kuṉṟa māka,
       vāriāṟukaḷ vaṟantu ēḻkaliṅka mumuṭaṉ
               cōriāṟumva yaṅka,
       paṇiniḻal paṭaeḻum parumaṉuk kāṭupōy
               aṟukuṟaik kāṭu tuṉṟa,
       pāṭivī ṭukaḷpeṟum pantarpōl eṅkaṇum
               kaḻukunaṟ pantar pampa,
       taṇiniḻal kuṭaiyoṭum paṭaiyoṭum pōyimak
               kulakirip pukkuo ḷikkum
       tāṉaimaṉ ṉavarmaṟin taṉameṉap paṭitaṭik
               koṭikaḷ tō ṟunta ṉatupēr
       aṇiniḻal pōravā ceṟṟakōṉ ivaṉuṭaṉ,
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli!āṭa vāvē!

   69. kuṉṟukaḷ piṇakkuṉṟāka, āṟukaḷ kuruti āṟukaḷāka, peruṅkāṭukaḷ
   kuṟaikkāṭukaḷāy aḻiya, pāṭi vīṭukaḷiṉ pantarkaḷ kaḻukukaḷ vaṭṭamiṭum
   pantatarākat tōṟṟōṭiya pakaimaṉṉar mīṇṭu vantu aṭikaḷaip paṇinta aḷavil
   pōrāṟṟum viruppiṉai nīkkiṉavaṉ ivaṉ.
   taṉṉai vaḻipaṭuvārkku aruḷ ceyyum tāṉam ippāṭalil kāṇka. vāri - veḷḷam;
   vaṟantu - nīr aṟṟu; parumaṉukkāṭu - paruttu nirampiya kāṭu; maṟintaṉam
   - mīṇ ṭaṉam; paṭi taṭik koṭi - tāḻtti vaṇaṅkum koṭi. (3)
   ------------------------------------
   70.
   karavukōḷ iṉṟiniṉṟu amutiṭum pōtakattu
               ulakammeyt taṇta miḻkkum
       kālakalai ............... īreṭṭum nīḷa,niṉ
               kaḻaliṉil karuṇai vaittu,
       paravukōḷ arivainum pāyalē mīteḻuntu
               oḻikaeṉac cikara kōṭip
       paṭippatti mattutti vilmatti mattuutirt
               tavaṉivaṉ, tāyum appaṇṭu
       iravucēr cōtiyiṉ mīteḻum eḻucciyil
               aṭikkaṭi yēe tirttē,
       eyiṟupukku uruvavāy akavaiyiṉ kaṭitiṉil
               pukamaṭuttu, eḻavi ḻuṅkum
       aravukōḷ vīṭukoṇ ṭavaṉivaṉ; ivaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   70. karavukōḷ - maṟaittu vaittal; kāya - veḷippaṭutta; pattimattu utti
   - aṉpuṭaiya iṇakkam; mattimam - naṭu.
   niṉ amutakalai īreṭṭaiyum tamiḻukkum vaḻaṅkiyatāl uṉṉiṭam karuṇai
   vaittu, uṉṉaik kavara varum aravu kōḷ viṭuttavaṉ ivaṉ.
   ituvum tāṉam. (4)
   -----------------------------------
   71.
   pāyiram puṉaitamiḻt teṉticaip pulavarum
               vaṭakalaip pulamai yōrum
       paṇpaṭā, uṭaicevik kaṇpaṭā uṟukaḷattu
               aracartam aṭikaḷ tōṟum
       pōyiruntu oḻivarum paṭaroḻintu uvaḷvarum
               kavikaiyāṉ rāca rācaṉ,
       porutakā liṅkaraip pōr tolaittu avarkaḷpiṉ
               pōyataṉ cēṉai yālē,
       vēyiraṅku aṭavipōy veḷipaṭap pakalavaṉ
               veñcilā vaṭṭam ellām
       veyilpaṭap paṭanerup pupporup puccuṭar
               viṭuneṭuñ cikaika ḷāṉa
       āyiram poṭipaṭac ceṟṟakōṉ, ivaṉuṭaṉ,
               ampuli! āṭavāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   71. pulavar pāṭum veṟṟiyum ceṅkōlumuṭaiya irācarācaṉ. kāliṅkarai veṉṟu
   taṉ cēṉaiyāl avaratu nāṭṭaic ceṟṟavaṉākiya ivaṉuṭaṉ āṭavā.
   itu vārāviṭiṉ oṟuppaṉ eṉṟaliṉ, taṇṭam. (5)
   -------------------------------
   72.
   pallavēn .... . tarumpariya āyiram
               paḵṟalaip pakaia rāvum,
       paṇṭutāṉ pāramē koṇṭupōy eṟivatuōr
               paravaiyum tavira, niṉṉaip
       pullanē mippirāṉ vantaḻait taṉaṉ;iṉip
               pukaviḻuṅ kiyume ṭuttum
       porupaṭaikku añcalnī; eṉṉapayam? eṉṉavum,
               piṉṉum,ap purava laṉtāṉ
       nallaṉō? kaṭalkaṭaintu amutukoṇ ṭaruḷumnāḷ,
               nañcukāl kayiṟu, cūḻpoṉ
       nākamat tukku,nē rā,neṭun tūṇiṉukku
               uṉṉaiyē nāṭi naṭṭāṉ
       allaṉō? iṉṟumē iriyalpō kappeṟāy;
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   72. niṉṉaik kavarum pāmpum, nī oyvu koḷḷum kaṭalum tavirumāṟu niṉṉait
   taḻuvutaṟku innēmippirāṉ aḻaikkiṟāṉ. avaṉ nēmiyāl uṉakkut tuyar nērātu.
   avaṉē amutu kaṭainta kālattē vācuki nāṇkoṇṭu mantaramalaiyāl
   kaṭaiyumpōtu, mattiṉai iṇaikkum tūṇukku uṉṉai naṭṭup perumai ceytavaṉ.
   iṉṉum avaṉai viṭuttu nīṅkātiruppiṉ ciṟapparuḷuvāṉ. nallaṉō - nallaṉ
   allaṉō.
   itu tāṉam. (6)
   -------------------------------------
   73.
   ēṉainī veṇṭalaik koṇṭalvaṇ ṇavaṉ,ivaṉ
               ēḻperuṅ koṇṭal vaṇṇaṉ;
       iravilē taṉiviḷaṅ kuvatunī, ippirāṉ
               iravumel liyumvi ḷaṅkum;
       pāṉumītu eḻumuṉakku oṉṟumaṇ ṭalam,ivaṟku
               ēḻumaṇ ṭalam;u tittup
       paṭivatuōr paravaiyiṉ, ivaṉukantu ēḻperum
               paravaiyum paṭiya vantāṉ;
       mēlnilā viyaiṭattu orupoḻiṟku iṟaivaṉnī,
               ēḻpoḻiṟku iṟaivaṉ ikkō;
       mēruviṉ tikiricūḻ varuvai nī, ivaṉtikiri
               varuvatuēḻ veṟpum aṉṟē;
       ‘āṉapōtu ivaṉainām aṭaivatē tarumam'eṉṟu
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   73. nī veṇkoṇṭal vaṇṇaṉ; ivaṉ ēḻkoṇṭal vaṇṇaṉ. nī iravil māttiram oḷi
   viṭuvāy; ivaṉ irunēramum viḷaṅkuvāṉ. uṉakkuc cañcarippataṟku oru
   maṇṭalamē uṇṭu; ivaṉukku ēḻu maṇṭalam uṇṭu. nī tōṉṟip piṉ kuḷippatu orē
   kaṭalil; ivaṉ ēḻ kaṭalilum kuḷippavaṉ. nī orē ulakiṟkut talaivaṉ; ivaṉ
   ēḻu ulakaṅkaḷukkum talaivaṉ. nī mērumalaiyaiyē cuṟṟi varuvāy; ivaṉ
   tikiri ēḻumalaikaḷaiyum cuṟṟum. ātaliṉ niṉṉiṉ mikkāṉākiya ivaṉai
   aṭaivatē ēṟṟatu.
   itu pētam. (7)
   ----------------------------------
   74.
   mēlivaṉ tikiriyum toṭṭatē toṭṭatuivaṉ
               veyyatōḷ virutam aṉṟō?
       veḷiyayām uruvareṉṟu oḻiyavē moḻiyilivaṉ
               muṉṉavaṉ veḷiyaṉ aṉṟō?
       pālilventu eḻummuṭaikku uṭaitiyēl muṉpuivaṉ
               paḷḷikoḷvu ataṉil aṉṟō?
       paṭarmaṟuk kavaruti nīcuṭar maṟuppoti(i)vaṉ
               paintuḻāy mārpaṉ aṉṟō?
       kōlivantu utavum eṭ ṭeṭṭuivaṉ kalaikaḷuṉ
               kalaikaḷī reṭṭil oṉṟaik
       koḷuvanī verukkoḷvatu ēṉ?ataṉ tēṉiṉaik
               koḷiṉpakam koḷvaṉ allāl,
       āli(i)vaṉ koḷḷumō ? talamvarā, ivaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   74. ivaṉ kaiyil cakkaram vaittirukkiṟāṉē eṉṉiṉ, ataṉai ivaṉ tōḷ
   cumattal viratamākum. nī veṇṇiṟam paṟṟip perumitamuṟṟāl, ivaṉ aṇṇaṉ
   palarāmaṉum veḷḷai niṟantaṉē. nī ivaṉiṭam pālnāṟṟam vīcumeṉa niṉaikkiṉ,
   nī piṟanta pāṟkaṭalilēyē ivaṉ paḷḷikoḷkiṟāṉ. nī kariya maṟuviṉai
   vaittuḷḷāy. ivaṉ mārpil oḷi vīcum srīvatcam eṉṟa maccam uḷḷatu. ivaṉ
   aṟupattu nāṉku kalaikaḷaiyum uṭaiyavaṉ. attakaiyavaṉ uṉ kalaikaḷ
   patiṉāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟaip paṟṟiṉāl atu paṟṟi nī añcuvatu ēṉ? uṉ kalaiyiṉ
   amutattaikkoḷḷum iṉpattait tavira uṉ kuḷircciyai ivaṉ kavarāṉ. ātaliṉ
   ivaṉuṭaṉ āṭavā.
   vārā eṉpatu vikāramāy niṉṟatu.
   matiyiṉum ivaṉai mēmpaṭuttuvatāl, ituvum pētam. (8)
   ------------------------------------
   75.
   ceñcolam puli!ivaṉ pukaḻnilā akilamum
               ceṉṟuniṉ ṟatu teḷintum,
       tiruvanā yakaṉivaṉ kuṭainilā uyirtoṟum
               karuṇaivī cuvatu kaṇṭum
       cañcalam perukaniṉ ceṟunilā viṭutiyaic
               ceṟinilā veṉṟu viṭṭāṉ,
       taṟukaṇaṉā kuvaṉivaṉ; paravaiōr ēḻaiyum
               taṉikkoḻup pittatu ikkō;
       mañcualam piyacilampu ēḻiṉum cilacilā
               talamuruk kuti;i vaṉpū
       valaiyamē ḻiṉumeḻum kamalarā cikaḷaiyē
               vantucī ṟuti;i vaṟṟāl,
       añcal;uṉ ciṟumaiyāl avaipoṟut taṉaṉivaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   75. ivaṉ pukaḻnilā ulaku muḻutum vīcukiṟatu; ivaṉ kuṭainilā uyirtoṟum
   karuṇai poḻikiṟatu; niṉ oḷiyai ivaṉ taṉ oḷiyāl veṉṟavaṉ; ēḻu
   kaṭalkaḷaiyum ciṟakkacceyta ivaṉ añcāneñcamuṭaiyavaṉ; taṉ malaikaḷil
   kaṟkaḷai uṉ kiraṇaṅkaḷāl urukkiyum, taṉ ulakil tōṉṟum tāmaraikaḷai nī
   kōpittum ceyyum ceyalkaḷai ivaṉ poṟuttu aḻaikkiṉṟāṉ.
   vārā viṭiṉ oṟuppaṉ eṉṟaliṉ, taṇṭam. (9)
   -----------------------------------
   76.
   eṇticā mukamaṭaṅ kalumivaṉ kaṭataṭattu
               eṇkaḷiṟṟu āla yamkāṇ;
       eṇpilaṅ kaḷumivaṉ tiṟaiyiṭā aracarukku
               iṭuciṟaip pēri ṭamkāṇ;
       maṇṭumēḻ kaṭalivaṉ tiruvoṭum paṭiyoṭum
               paṭiyumañ caṉama ṭukkāṇ;
       mallalēḻ poḻilumēḻ puvaṉiyum pavaṉipōy
               makiḻumnan taṉava ṉamkāṇ;
       viṇṭavēy muttuaṟā ēḻvilaṅ kalumivaṉ
               vēḻavā rippu lamkāṇ;
       mērumāl varaiivaṉ koṭuvarikku iṭṭatuōr
               vīraciṅ kāta ṉamkāṇ;
       aṇṭakō ḷakaiivaṉ kavikaikāṇ; ātalāl,
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ! cayataraṉ! maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   76. eṭṭut ticai ellaiyum ivaṉuṭaiya paṭṭattu yāṉaiyiṉ ālayam; eṭṭuk
   kukaikaḷum ivaṉ pakaimaṉṉar ciṟai vaikkappaṭum iṭam; ēḻkaṭalkaḷum ivaṉ
   tirumakaḷōṭu nīr viḷaiyāṭum maṭu; ēḻpoḻilum ēḻ ulakum ivaṉ ulāppōntu
   makiḻum nantaṉavaṉam; mūṅkil muttukkaḷai uṭaiya ēḻmalaikaḷum ivaṉ
   yāṉaikaḷ taṅkumiṭam; mērumālvarai ivaṉ muttiraiyiluḷḷa pulikkuc
   ciṅkātaṉam; aṇṭattu ucci ivaṉuṭaiya veṇkuṭai.
   matiyaiviṭap paṉ maṭaṅku cōḻaṉ ēṟṟam kūṟaliṉ, itu pētam. (10)
   ----------------------------------
   77.
   maṭimaiyāl vaṟituirātu, aṅkavaṅ kamkaliṅ
               kamkuliṅ kamtri kattam
       māḷuvam cōṇakam pappaḷam koppaḷam
               makatameṉṟu ivaiyum, maṟavam
       kaṭiyumō ṉaṅkaḷām aracuvēr aṟaeṟintu,
               arumaṇam cāva kamvaṅ
       kāḷam īḻamkaṭā ramtavāc cīṉameṉṟu
               yāvaiyum kaippaṭutti,
       kuṭimaiyāḷ aracarē ākaviṭṭu, avarcikara
               makuṭakō ṭikaḷil vaikkum
       koṭikaḷē vaṭaticaik kaṅkaiyait teṉticaik
               koṇṭueḻap paṇṭu koṇṭu,aṅku
       aṭimaiyā ḷumpirāṉ makaṉmakaṉ, ivaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   77. cōmpiyirātu aṅkam mutaliya nāṭṭu aracarkaḷai vērōṭu aḻittu,
   arumaṇam mutaliya nāṭukaḷaik kaippaṟṟi, mīṇṭum ammaṉṉarkaḷaiyē
   avvannāṭṭu aracarākki, avarkaḷaik koṇṭu kaṅkainīrait tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuk
   koṇarac ceyta aracaṉ paramparaiyiṉaṉ ivaṉ.
   ivaṉum niṉṉai oṟuttup paṇikoḷvaṉ eṉṟaliṉ taṇṭam. (11)
   -------------------------------------
   78.
   vīriyaṉ, aṉupamaṉ, vāṉavaṉ, mīṉavaṉ,
               viṉiyataṉ, vilvi ṉōtaṉ,
       vīrakaṇ ṭīravaṉ, vīranā rāyaṇaṉ,
               vimalaṉ,rā cāti rācaṉ,
       cūriyaṉ, viyākaraṉ, tuṅkaama laṉ,varaṉ,
               turakavit tiyāvi ṉōtaṉ,
       cōḻavam cātipaṉ, cālaē kōpamaṉ,
               uttuṅkaṉ,ut tuṅka tuṅkaṉ,
       nēriyaṉ, pañcavaṉ, cēralaṉ, cempiyaṉ,
               nittiyakī tappira mōkaṉ,
       nirupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉuparaṉ, kuṇaparaṉ,
               nityakal yāṇa pūṣaṇaṉ,
       āriyaṉ, rācarā cāriyaṉ, ivaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!
       aṉupamaṉ, cayataraṉ, maṉukulā tipaṉuṭaṉ
               ampuli! āṭa vāvē!

   78. vīriyaṉ - āṟṟaluṭaiyavaṉ; viṉītaṉ - cāṉṟōriṭam paṇivuṭaiyavaṉ;
   kaṇṭīravaṉ - ciṅkam pōlpavaṉ; viyākraṉ - pulipōlpavaṉ; tuṅka -
   paricuttamāṉa; turakavityā - kutiraiyēṟṟam; ēkōpamaṉ - oppaṟṟavaṉ;
   uttuṅkaṉ - uyarntavaṉ; uttuṅkatuṅkaṉ - uyarnta tūyavaṉ; nēriyaṉ -
   cōḻaṉ; pañcavaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉ; piramōkaṉ - viruppamuṭaiyavaṉ; kītam -
   icaippāṭal; āriyaṉ - vaṇaṅkat takkavaṉ; kalyāṇa pūṣaṇaṉ - maṅkalattai
   aṇipavaṉ.
   uyarcci kūṟaliṉ pētam.
   ippāṭalil immaṉṉaṉ peyarkaḷ palavum kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka. (12)
   ------------------------------------------

                         8. ciṟupaṟaip paruvam.

   79.
   alaiumiḻa muḻakkumaṭu naṭunaṭu pukkuacurar
       kulaiyamalai yappeyarum iṟaiva!ku marittuṟaiva!
       malaiyamalai veṟpa!tamiḻ maturaimatu rakkalaiva!
       cilaiya!maṉu cakratara! ciṟupaṟai erukkukavē. 1

   ciṟu paṟaiyaik koṭṭumāṟu kuḻantaiyai vēṇṭutal.
   79. muḻakkummaṭu - olikkum kaṭal. ivaṉ acurarai aḻittamai kula muṉṉōr
   paṟṟiya purāṇac ceyti. maturam - iṉimai; kalaivaṉ - kalaiñaṉ; cilaiyaṉ
   - vil ēntiyavaṉ; erukkutal - taṭṭi oli eḻupputal. (1)
   --------------------------------
   80.
   naṟṟavar aṟukuṟaiyāl nakraa ṇaiviṭuvāy!
       kaṟṟavar kuṭipuṭaicūḻ kaṟpaka iḷavaṉamē!
       ceṟṟavar kuṭikeṭavē ceppiṭu maḻakaḷiṟē!
       koṟṟavaṉ makaṉmakaṉē! koṭṭuka ciṟupaṟaiyē.

   80. tavattōr kuṟaiyai aṟukkap paṭukkaiyai viṭṭu irāmaṉāka vantavaṉ.
   ivaṉ vekuḷiccol kēṭṭa aḷavil pakaivar kuṭi keṭṭatu. nakram - mutalai.
   paḷḷikoṇṭa pāṟkaṭalum kaṭalātaliṉ kaṭalukku uḷḷa aṭai puṇarkkappaṭṭatu.
   (2)
   ----------------------------------
   81.
   niṉṟavaṉ, nikarvaṭakā liṅkar nerukkiyapōr
       veṉṟavaṉ; aracarpirāṉ, vikrama cōḻa!valam
       ceṉṟavaṉ, iravi,pacum puravici vantueḻupōr
       koṉṟavaṉ, maṉucaritā ! koṭṭuka ciṟupaṟaiyē.

   81. niṉṟavaṉ - nilaipeṟṟa pukaḻuṭaiyavaṉ. iravikulattavaṉai iravi
   eṉṟaḻaittuḷḷār. kutiraippaṭai kōpitteḻum pōril pakaivaraik koṉṟavaṉ.
   (3)
   ---------------------------------
   82.
   valavarai veṉṟupora, maṉumuta lākavaru
       nilavarai ēḻuṭaiya, nīḷkaṭal ēḻuṭaiyāy!
       pulavarai ēṟaviṭāp pukarmuka māpoḻiyum
       kulavarai ēḻuṭaiyāy koṭṭuka ciṟupaṟaiyē.

   82. valavar - pōril valla vīrar; pulavarai ēṟaviṭā - kuṟunilamaṉṉar
   tamatākak koṇṭu taṅkiyiruttal iyalāta. yāṉaikaḷ palavāka irukkum
   malaikaḷaip 'pukar mukamāp poḻiyum kulavarai ' eṉṟār. (4)
   --------------------------------------
   83.
   nāvala ṉē!kiṭaiyā navamaṇi maṇṭapamniṉ
       pāvala ṉēkavarap paṇṭupa ṇittavaṉē!
       ōvala ṉēeḻupēy orupuṭai yākakkai
       kōvala ṉē!apayā ! koṭṭuka ciṟupaṟaiyē.

   83. kiṭaiyā - eḷitil peṟa iyalāta; muṉṉōṉ pāvalaṉukku navamaṇi maṇṭapam
   paricilāka aḷittamai 14ām pāṭalilum kāṇka.
   pāl koṭukka eḻunta pēyākiya pūtaṉai uyir nīṅki orupuṭai kiṭakkumaḷavum
   taṉ ceyal nīṅkātu pāl kuṭitta kaṇṇaṉ ceyal kūṟappaṭṭatu. (5)
   -------------------------------
   84.
   viṭaipperu māṉ,mutirā veṇpiṟai cūṭiyaceñ
       caṭaipperu māṉ,natiyait tantaku lattalaivā!
       puṭaippati ṉālulakum potumaivi ṭappotiyum
       kuṭaipperumāṉ matalāy!koṭṭuka ciṟupaṟaiyē

   84. viṭaipperumāṉ, caṭaipperumāṉ - civaperumāṉ; talaivaṉ - pakīrataṉ;
   potumai - piṟarukkum urimaiyātal. (6)
   ------------------------------------------
   85.
   perumakaṉ mutaltalaivā! piṟaiperitu uvakkummutal
       marumakaṉ malaittalaivā ! maḻaivaḻi varuttavarum
       urumukil muḻakkameṉa oliviṭu kaṭakkaḷiṟṟut
       tirumakaṉ aruṭciṟuvā! ciṟupaṟai muḻakkukavē.

   85. piṟai uvakkum marumakaṉ - pāṇṭiyaṉ; uru - karuniṟam; tirumakaṉ -
   vikkiramaṉ. (7)
   -----------------------------------

                            9. ciṟṟil paruvam

   86.
   collummaṉa mumceyalum nallaperu māḷē!
               cūriyaṉ viḷaṅkavaru cōḻakula tuṅkā!
       villumiru keṇṭaiyum naṭuṅka,naṭu vēniṉ
               vempuli iruntatuoru mēruvaṭa veṟpā!
       vellumira vumpakalum vālmaṇi koḻittup
               pilattiṉum vicumpiṉum nilattiṉum eṭuttuc
       cellummika vēkanati kaṅkaikoṭu pōtum
               ceṅkaia payā!ematu ciṟṟilcitai yēlē.

   ciṟṟil paruvam
   ciṟumiyar 'yām iḻaikkum maṇaṟciṟṟillaṅkaḷaik kālkaḷāl citaikkātē' eṉṟu
   kuḻantaiyiṭam vēṇṭutal.
   86. pilam - pātāḷam; vicumpu - tēvar ulakam; 'kaṅkai koṭu pōtum ceṅkai
   apayaṉ' eṉap pakīrataṉ ceyal ivaṉ mēl ēṟṟappaṭṭatu. (1)
   ---------------------------------------
   87.
   mallaiyil vaḻaṅkumiḷa ēṟumutu kañcaṉ
               vañcavaya vēṟumaṭi yattaḻuvum mallā!
       mullaiyil veṟuñcila tuḻāykamaḻ mukuntā!
               muṉpu oru valampuri muḻakku mukilvaṇṇā!
       ellaiyil aṉantapala vētacura kītattu
               icaittapala tāṉavaḻi, iṭṭumeḻa viṭṭum
       tillaiyil naṭañceykama laṅkaḷai vaḷaikkum
               cintaiapa yā!ematu ciṟṟilcitai yēlē.

   87. mallai - vaḷamuṭaiya kālipuram; mutukañcaṉ - vañcaṉaiyāl mutirnta
   kamcaṉ; vayavēṟu - valiya tantiram; muṉpu - pārata yutta kālattil (42).
   ivaṉ tillaip perumāṉiṭam koṇṭa ārākkātal kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kamalaṅkaḷ -
   civaperumāṉ tiruvaṭikaḷ; ivai vēta olikkēṟpa naṭippaṉa. (2)
   --------------------------------------
   88.
   pūakam niṟaintavaṉi taikkumaṇa vāḷā !
               pāṉṉi yoṭu kavutamai taruṅkuṭa poruppā!
       kūvakar viṭumkaḷiṟu aḻittuulaku aḷikkum
               koṭaikkai apayā!kula natikkumari koṇkā!
       pāvakaṉ vaḷaittueḻu kaliṅkam viḻuṅkap
               pakaṭṭaṇi tuṇittuoru perumparaṇi koḷḷum
       cēvakaṉ apaṅkaṉaka ḷaṅkaṉmata lāy!niṉ
               cēvaṭi kaḷālematu ciṟṟilcitai yēlē.

   88. vaṉitai – tirumakaḷ; kavutamai - kōtāviri; tikkumari - natikaḷaik
   koṇṭa kumarippakutiyākiya pāṇṭiya nāṭu; koṇkaṉ - kaṇavaṉ, neytalnilat
   talaivaṉ; pāvakaṉ - akkiṉi; pakaṭu - āṇyāṉai; cēvakaṉ - vīraṉ; apaṅkaṉ
   - kuṟaivillātavaṉ; akaḷaṅkaṉ - maṟu illātavaṉ. (3)
   ----------------------------
   89.
   muṉmitilai viliṟa murittuicai tarittāy!
               muḻaṅkukaṭal ēḻummuṉi yumpaṭi muṉintāy!
       iṉmatura muttamiḻum nāṉmaṟaiyum muṉṉāḷ
               eṭuttamuki lē!etiri lātaperu māḷē!
       vaṉmatil ilaṅkaieyil aṉṟu;utiyar kōmāṉ
               makōtaieyil aṉṟu;matil maṇṇainakar aṉṟu;
       teṉmaturai aṉṟu;vaṭa kampiliyum aṉṟu;uṉ
               cēvaṭika ḷālematu ciṟṟilcitai yēlē.

   89. murittal - vaḷaittal; muṟittal - iru tuṇṭamākkutal. ivaṉ muṉṉōr
   āṭciyilum muttamiḻum nāṉmaṟaiyum ciṟantaṉa eṉpatu. vil muṟittal, kaṭal
   muṉital eṉpaṉa irāmaṉuṭaiya ceyalkaḷ. ilaṅkai, makōtai, maṇṉai,
   maturai, kampili eṉpaṉa cōḻarkaḷ kaippaṟṟiyaṉa. peruñceyal ceytaṟkuriya
   nī ciṟṟil citaittalākiya ciṟuceyal ceyyātē. (4).
   -------------------------------
   90.
   veyyakali eṉṉumoru pēroruva ṉumpōy
               vēlaipuka vē,muṟaiyiṉ mīḷaula kellām
       uyya,ura kamtaḷai oḻittu,akila lōkattu
               uyarntamaṉu viṉkulam uvantaperu māḷē!
       vaiyaka maṭantaiyum, niṉakkuamutam nāṇiṉ
               vāruti kaṭaintutara vantatiru mārpiṉ
       ceyyatiru maṅkaiyum, uvantuvaruṭu ampoṉ
               cēvaṭika ḷāl,ematu ciṟṟilcitai yēlē.

   90. kali kaṭintu, āticēṭaṉ pārattait tāṉ tāṅki ataṉ pāram pōkki,
   maṉuviṉ kulattu uvantu vanta tirumālē ivaṉ. pūtēviyum cītēviyum ivaṉ
   aṭikaḷai varuṭuvar. urakam - pāmpu; nāṇ - vācukiyākiya kaṭai kayiṟu;
   vāruti - (pāṟ) kaṭal. (5)
   -----------------------------------
   91.
   taṇṭilviḻi ñattueyil takarttu,uṭaiya muttuc
               calāpamuḻu tum,poṟaiyar cālaiyōṭu koṇṭāy!
       keṇṭivayi rattuoḷi tuḷumpupuṉam nērik
               kirippurava lā!irava larkkuuriya kiḷḷi!
       vaṇṭiṉnekiḻ tārvaḻuti paṇṭueḻuti vaikkum
               matakkayal citakki,oru valliyam iruttac
       ceṇṭilimai yakkiri tirittacaya tuṅkā!
               cēvaṭika ḷālematu ciṟṟil citaiyēlē.

   91. viḻiñattu matilai aḻittuc cēraruṭaiya kāntaḷūrccālaiyōṭu muttuk
   kuḷikkum tuṟaikaḷaiyum kaippaṟṟiṉāy. uṉ nērmalaiyil vayiraṅkaḷ oḷi
   vīcukiṉṟaṉa. nī vaḷḷalāyuḷḷāy. imayamalaiyaic ceṇṭāyutattāl vaṇakki,
   atil poṟitta pāṇṭiyartam mīṉpoṟiyai akaṟṟip pulippoṟi poṟittāy. citakki
   - poṟittatai aḻittu; valliyam - puli. (6)
   -----------------------------------
   92.
   cāvakam eṟintu,aru maṇamporutu cintat
               takarttu,malai yūriṉuru vappuricai taḷḷi,
       kōakam nekiḻntukulai yumpaṭi kaṭāram
               koḷḷumoru cōḻaṉmaru kā!kumari koṇkā!
       pāvakam niramputiru mālummala rōṉum
               parantapati ṉeṇkaṇaṉum vantupara vattaṉ,
       cēvakam niramputiru vītipuli yūril
               ceytaperu māṉmatalai! ciṟṟilcitai yēlē.

   92. cāvakam, arumaṇam, malaiyūr, kaṭāram ivai veṉṟa mutal irācēntiraṉ
   marapiṉaṉ ivaṉ. pāvakam - tūymai. tirumālum piramaṉum tēvakaṇaṅkaḷum
   vaḻipaṭa, taṉ vīrattāl peṟṟa celvaṅkoṇṭu vikkiramaṉ citamparattil
   tiruvīti amaittamai cuṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. (7)
   ipputtakattuḷ pakkam 43-il attiruppaṇi virivukaḷaik kāṇka.
   ---------------------------------
   93.
   murukkunilai yātatiru vāymalari ṉālum
               mūrinakai yālumaṭi yōmuyirai muṉṉi
       urukkumatu naṉṟu;taṉi uṅkaḷkavi kaikkīḻ
               uḷḷapati ṉālulakum uyntaṉa; piḻaittōm;
       irukkumutal āraṇa mutaṟkulamum, viṇmēl
               eḻupaka lavaṉkulamum eṉṟu,ulaku iṟaiñcum
       tirukkulam iraṇṭummika vāyira ..... .............
               cēvakaṉ viṭuṅkaḷiṟu! ciṟṟil citai yēlē.

   93. murukku - muḷ muruṅkai; mūri - perumai: patiṉālulakum kākkum nī,
   vāy malarālum muṟuvalālum em uyirai urukkumāṟu eṉṉa tavaṟu ceytōm?
   vētam kūṟum mutaṟkulamākiya kīḻaiccaḷukkiyar kulamum cūriyaṉ kulamākiya
   cōḻarkulamum ciṟakkat tōṉṟiyavaṉ nī. āyiraṅkaṇṇōṉām intiraṉaik
   kāḷaiyākki ivarnta kākuttaṉ ceyti ivaṉ mītēṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu pōlum.
   (8)
   -------------------------------------
   94.
   valampukalu māṟuaṟiya vilkoṭi neṭutēr
               vāṉavaṉum mīṉavaṉum vallavaṉum ōṭa,
       pilampuka muṉintakali kōpaṉaka ḷaṅkaṉ
               peṟṟakaḷi ṟē!etiri lātaperu māḷē!
       alampukaṭal ēḻummalai ēḻumnilam ēḻum
               alarntanati yāvaiyum āmivai aṉaittum
       cilampukaḷum uṭpaṭa aḷantu,ulaku aḷikkum
               cēvaṭika ḷāl,ematu ciṟṟilcitai yēlē.

   94. valam pukalumāṟu - taṉ veṟṟiyaik kuṟippiṭumāṟu; aṟiya - ulakattavar
   uṇara; kalikōpaṉ - vaṟumaiyai vekuṇṭavaṉ. ivaṉ
   tiruvikkiramaṉāyiruntakālai ulaku muḻutum aḷantāṉ eṉpatu. (9)
   -------------------------------------
   95.
   marukkamaḻum vaṭamaturai vaḷanakarkkum paḻaiyōm;
               vaṇtuvarai kaṭaluṭaṉē makiḻuṟanaṟ pukuntōm;
       irukkilvarum peruntakaikkum ettaṉaikkum paḻaiyōm;
               eyilvaraiyait toṭarppaṭutta mukilniḻal kīḻiruntōm;
       kurukkaaṭi maikkuṭiyāy varukuṭikaḷ aṭiyōm;
               koṟṟavaṉē! kūttāṭu nāyakaṉē! uṅkaḷ
       tirukkulanā yakipukutap pukuntōmciṟ ṟaṭiyōm;
               ciṟṟaṭiyōm yāmiḻaikkum ciṟṟilcitai yēlē.

   95. ciṟumiyar ivaṟkup paḻaiya aṭiyavarāntiṟam kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
   vaṭamaturaiyilum, putitākak kaṭaliṉuḷ amaikkappaṭṭa tuvārakaiyilum
   paṇṭu iruntaṉar; vētam pukaḻum ipperumāṉukkup paḻaiya aṭiyavarkaḷāy,
   ivaṉ kōvarttaṉamalaiyaik kuṭaiyākap piṭikka akkuṭai niḻaṟkīḻ iruntaṉar;
   ivaṉ kula nāyakiyuṭaṉ ippiṟappeṭuttuḷḷaṉar. ittakaiya paḻaiya aṭiyavar
   tam ciṟṟillaic citaittal takātu eṉpatu. kūttu - kuṭakkūttu mutali
   kaṇṇaṉ naṭaṉaṅkaḷ. (10)
   --------------------------------
   96.
   curakuruvi ṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, curarnāṭu pukuntē,
               curapatikkum narapatikkum toṭuttueṭutta kavari
       aramakaḷir paṇimāṟa, yāṅkaḷpaṇi māṟa,
               aravaṇaiyaic cēvittōm; antikkāppu eṭuttōm;
       puravalaṟkum purantaraṟkum uṭaṉeṭuppa eṭutta
               poṟkalattōr maṉamtoṭarntōm; purantaraṟkum iṟaikkut
       tirumakaṉē! kūttāṭu nāyakaṉē! uṅkaḷ
               ciṟṟaṭiyōm yāmiḻaikkum ciṟṟil citaiyēlē.

   96. curapati - intiraṉ; narapati - curakuru eṉṉum cōḻaṉ. avaṉōṭu
   immakaḷir vāṉulakam cellum vāyppup peṟṟapōtu, aramakaḷir
   tēvēntiraṉukkuc ceyta kuṟṟēvalkaḷait tāṅkaḷum curakuruvukkuc ceyyum
   vāyppiṉaip peṟṟaṉar; tirumālai vantittu ālatti cuḻaṟṟiṉar. kākuttaṉ
   tēvēntiraṉaiyum paṇi koṇṭamaiyiṉ, cōḻaṉ purantaraṉukkum talaivaṉāyiṉāṉ.
   accōḻaṉ marapiṉaṉ ivaṉ. purantaraṉ - intiraṉ; iṟai- talaivaṉ. (11)
   -------------------------------

                           10. ciṟutērp paruvam

   97.
   māṟiya kalikku vaḻivilak kāka,
               paṭiyiṉuḷ maṉuvaram pāka,
       kūṟiya kaṭavuḷ kulakkaḷiṟu eṭṭum
               eṇticai yiṉum kuṟik koḷḷa,
       ēṟiya kaliṅkamum ēḻumā rāṭṭamum
               ēḻarai ilakkamum iriyac
       cīṟiya apayaṉ! kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ !
               uruṭṭuka ciṟutērē.

   ciṟutērp paruvam
   viḷaiyāṭutalukkākac ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟiya tēriṉai uruṭṭi viḷaiyāṭumāṟu
   kuḻantaikkuk kūṟutal.
   97. kaliyiṉ vaḻiyai vilakki, maṉuneṟiyai vēliyiṭṭu, eṭṭutticaiyilum
   kaliṅkarum marāṭṭarum ēḻarai ilakkattārākiya iraṭṭarum ōṭumāṟu
   cīṟiyavaṉ apayaṉ. māṟiya - ulakoḻukkam māṟakkāraṇamāṉa kaṭavuḷ uṟaiyum
   aṭṭa kulavaraikaḷ eṉka. (1)
   -----------------------------------
   98.
   aṅkaṇmā ñālam orupuṭai potinta
               aṇikuṭai maṉukulā tipaṉē!
       eṅkaṉnā yaka!nam kulanati vaḷavaṉ
               irācarā caṉpeṟu mavuli
       veṅkaṇmāl yāṉai mutalaivāy muṭiyum
               vēlaivāy, vīṭukoṇ ṭaruḷum
       ceṅkaṇmāl! vaḷavaṉ! kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ!
               uruṭṭuka ciṟutērē

   98. ulakai orukuṭaikkīḻ āṇṭa maṉukulat talaivaṉ. ñālattait taṉkīḻ oru
   cār kavitta kuṭaiyeṉa, ataṉ perumai kūṟiṉār. kulanati vaḷavaṉ -
   kāviriyiṉ pōkkai oḻuṅkupaṭuttip payaṉkoṇṭa cōḻaṉ. ‘irācarācaṉ
   peṟumavuliyai avaṉ vaḻi vantu cūṭiya ceṅkaṇ mālām vaḷavaṉ' eṉak
   kūṭṭuka. tirumāl kacēntiraṉaik kātta ceyal kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa tu. (2)
   --------------------------------
   99.
   pūṭṭiya cilaiyum vayirppitta aramum
               ceyirppitta kēḷvarai, pōṉṟu
       kāṭṭiya kaḷiṟum kiḷarvitta viṭaiyum
               taṉittaṉi pilattil vīḻntu aḻunta,
       nāṭṭiya ........... ..... ........ neruṅka
               nāliru ticaiyiṉum vicaiyam
       tīṭṭiya ceṉṉi! kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ!
               uruṭṭuka ciṟutērē.

   99. cilai - cērarpoṟi; vayirppitta - kaṟuvu koḷvitta aram - aram pōṉṟa
   kūriya vāyaiyuṭaiya ilaikaḷaiyuṭaiya vēmpu; itu pāṇṭiyar aṭaiyāḷa
   mālai. cērarum pāṇṭiyarum cōḻaṉ kōpattukku āḷāṉapōtu, patuṅkutaṟku
   naṭpāy amaintaṉa malaikaḷē. ēṉaiya yāṉaippoṟi viṭaippoṟiyuṭaiya maṉṉar
   pilam pukak kulōttuṅkaṉ eṇticaikaḷilum taṉ vīrattait tīṭṭiṉāṉ. pōṉṟu -
   avarai pōṉṟu. (3)
   ----------------------------------
   100.
   tarittaṉaṉ cēṭaṉ taḷarntaṉaṉ eṉṟu
               talattiṉait tāṅkuva tāka
       varittava ṉiṉmuṉ vantakōṉ aṉurai
               mātavaṉ ... pa... ... kaṇṭu vayiṟu
       karittiyal utiyar ... ..... ..................
               kaivarai iḻintamai muṉkaṇṭu
       cirittavaṉ matalai! kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ!
               uruṭṭuka ciṟutērē.

   100. āticēṭaṉ acaivu kaṇṭu tāṉē ulakait tāṅkiyavaṉ. cērar
   yāṉaiyiliruntu iṟaṅki ōṭiyataik kaṇṭu cirittavaṉ ākiya vikkiramaṉ makaṉ
   ivaṉ. (4)
   ------------------------------------
   101.
   āṟṟiya muracāl vaṭapula vēntar
               añciṉar kāṇum eṉṟu aṟaintu
       cāṟṟiya vaḷavaṉ cayaṅkoṇṭa cōḻaṉ
               ... ... ... ... talattu
       māṟṟiya kaliyāṉ mayaṅkiya taṅkaḷ
               maṉuvaiyum maṟaiyaiyum vaḷarkkat
       tōṟṟiya ceṉṉi! kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ!
               uruṭṭuka ciṟutērē.

   101. muracoli kēṭṭa aḷavilēyē vaṭapula vēntar añciōṭa, pōrait tavirttu,
   vaṟumaiyai māṟṟi, maṉunītiyaiyum vētattaiyum vaḷartta irācātirācaṉ
   marapiṉaṉ ivaṉ. (5)
   ----------------------------------
   102.
   eṉṟavaṉ muṭiyum, vaḷarcilai vēmpum,
               maturaiyum, utakaiyum, maṟṟu
       niṉṟaveñ cilaiyum, kiṭantaceṅ kayalum
               neruppuṇṭu, nīṟunīṟu āka;
       kuṉṟavaṉ potiyil koṟṟavaṉ muṉiyap
               putu .... ..... ... ... kuṭakaṭal kuḷippac
       ceṉṟavaṉ matalai! kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ!
               uruṭṭuka ciṟutērē.

   102. eṉṟavaṉ - ēṉṟavaṉ eṉpataṉ kuṟukkam. pōrai ēṟṟa pāṇṭiyaṉuṭaiya
   muṭi, cilai, vēmpu, maturai, kayal eṉpaṉavum cēraṉuṭaiya utakai, vil
   mutaliyaṉavum tīppaṭṭuc cāmparāyiṉa. ivveṟṟikkup piṉ mēlkaṭalil
   nīrāṭiyavaṉ vikkiramaṉ. muṉital - veṟuttu(ppōrai) nīṅkutal. (6)
   ------------------------------------
   103.
   pāṭiya poykai kaḷavaḻi kēṭṭu,
               paṭippaṭi yē aṭṭu eṟintu
       vāṭiya poṟaiya ṉaicciṟai mīṭṭa
               maṉṉavaṉ orutiru marukaṉ!
       kōṭiyar, pulavar, viṟaliyar, pāṇar,
               kulakiri kaḷumkuṟai niṟaiyat
       tēṭiya nitiyam! kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉ!
               uruṭṭuka ciṟutērē.

   103. poykaiyār pāṭiya kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu eṉṟa nūlaik kēṭṭut tāṉ veṉṟu
   ciṟaippiṭitta cēramāṉ kaṇaikkāl irumpoṟai muṉpōla aracaṉākumāṟu ciṟai
   mīṭṭa ceṅkaṇāṉ marapiṉaṉ ivaṉ. kūttar mutaliyōrukkut tēṭi vaitta celvak
   kuviyal pōlvāṉ. kōṭiyar - kūttar. (7)
   ----------------------------
   104.
   oruvā .... ... ... yeṉa niṉṟōr paṇṭurakāraṅkam
               kālkoṇṭu muṟikkai ... ... ... vāy piḷaviṭṭavaṉ
       maṉṉavar maṉṉa ṉ viṭaiyum ........ ...
               taṉittaṉi pilattil vīḻntaḻunta, .... .... .........
       neruṅka, nāliru ticaiyiṉum vicaiyam
               tīṭṭiya ceṉṉi! uruṭṭuka ciṟutērē. 8

   104. pakaivar pilattil vīḻntu aḻiya, eṇticaiyiṉum taṉ veṟṟiyait tīṭṭiya
   kulōttuṅkaṉē! nī ciṟutēr uruṭṭuka.
   urakāraṅkam - pāmpiṉ talaiyiṭam. kāḷiṅkaṉ talaiyil naṭamiṭṭamai, mā vāy
   piḷantamai, viṭaikaḷai aṭarttamai eṉṉum kaṇṇaṉ ceyalkaḷ ivaṉmītu ēṟṟik
   kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (8)
   --------------------------------------------
